{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"hoods","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A covering for the head, usually attached to a larger garment such as a jacket or cloak.","A head covering placed on falcons to inhibit their vision."],"topics":["falconry","hobbies","hunting","lifestyle"]},{"glosses":["A covering for the head, usually attached to a larger garment such as a jacket or cloak.","A head and neck covering placed on horses to protect against insects and sunlight, to slow coat growth and for warmth."],"synonyms":[{"word":"blinder"}],"topics":["equestrianism","hobbies","horses","lifestyle","pets","sports"]},{"glosses":["A distinctively colored fold of material, representing a university degree."]},{"glosses":["An enclosure that protects something, especially from above."]},{"glosses":["Particular parts of conveyances","A soft top of a convertible car or carriage."],"tags":["UK"],"topics":["automotive","transport","vehicles"]},{"glosses":["Particular parts of conveyances","The hinged cover over the engine of a motor vehicle, known as a bonnet in other countries."],"synonyms":[{"word":"cowl"},{"word":"motorhood"},{"word":"bonnet"}],"tags":["Canada","US"],"topics":["automotive","transport","vehicles"]},{"glosses":["Particular parts of conveyances","A cover over the engine, driving machinery or inner workings of something."],"raw_tags":["in the phrase \"under the hood\""],"tags":["broadly","especially"]},{"glosses":["Particular parts of conveyances","A metal covering that leads to a vent to suck away smoke or fumes."]},{"glosses":["Particular parts of conveyances","One of the endmost planks (or, one of the ends of the planks) in a ship’s bottom at bow or stern, that fits into the rabbet. (These, when fit into the rabbet, resemble a hood (covering).)"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["Various body parts","An expansion on the sides of the neck typical for many elapids e.g. the Egyptian cobra (Naja haje) and Indian cobra (Naja naja)."]},{"glosses":["Various body parts","The osseous or cartilaginous marginal extension behind the back of many a dinosaur such as a ceratopsid and reptiles such as Chlamydosaurus kingii."],"synonyms":[{"word":"frill"}],"tags":["colloquial"]},{"glosses":["Various body parts","In the human hand, over the extensor digitorum, an expansion of the extensor tendon over the metacarpophalangeal joint (the extensor hood syn. dorsal hood syn. lateral hood)"]},{"glosses":["Various body parts","The prepuce; the foreskin or clitoral hood."],"tags":["colloquial"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/hʊd/","tags":["Australia","General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[hʊ̈d]","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"[hɪ̈d]","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/hɵd/","tags":["British","Southern","Standard"]},{"ipa":"/hʉd/","tags":["Northern-Ireland","Scotland"]}],"upstream_line":251893,"upstream_offset":1250342966,"upstream_record_sha256":"c07c5f0dcf3dd41428fc1f3d89f204f94839fb4c05f0366bc8854f4ede2a6b70","word":"hood"}
